L^{\infty}\times L^{2}\times L^{2}
estimate. By (
3.15
), and (
3.19
), we obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}_{m,\,\mathbf{k}}|\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{[2H(n)+1]{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k_{2}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{N(n)}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(n+1)-N(0)}

(6.39)

for
n=1,2
. Now the RHS of (
6.39
) is summable. Analogously, for the case
\min(\mathbf{k})=k_{2}
, we use
L^{2}\times L^{2}\times L^{\infty}
estimate. Then (
3.20
) yield that, for
n=0,1,2
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}_{m,\,\mathbf{k}}|\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{[2H(n)+H(1)+\frac{1}{2}]{\delta}m}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(n+1)+2}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{N(n)-N(0)},

(6.40)

which finishes (
6.37
) for
n=1,2
.

The estimates (
6.39
) and (
6.40
) do not cover (
6.37
) when
n=3
. To handle this case, we use the integration by parts in time and obtain (
6.33
) and (
6.34
). Since
2^{\max(\mathbf{k})}\sim 2^{k_{1}}
, the second estimate in (
6.35
) guarantees the high frequency sum for (
6.33
) and the sum for the cases that
\partial_{s}
falls on
\phi_{\theta,\mathcal{L}}
and
V_{\mu,\mathcal{L},{\theta_{2}}}
in (
6.34
). To estimate remainder case, one can utilize (
6.36
) for the high frequency sum. This completes the proof of (
6.37
).
∎

6.4.
Bulk estimate on Maxwell part

Let us consider the remaining case of Maxwell part:

\displaystyle\int_{I_{m}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3+3}}q_{m}(s)|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\langle\xi\right\rangle^{2N(n)}\left\langle\widehat{\psi_{\theta_{1},{\mathcal{L}}_{1}}}(s,\eta),{\alpha}_{\mu}\widehat{\psi_{\theta_{2},{\mathcal{L}}_{2}}}(s,\xi+\eta)\right\rangle\overline{\widehat{W_{\mu,\mathcal{L},\theta^{\prime}}}}(s,\xi)d\eta d\xi ds.

(6.41)

Compared to
\mathcal{K}_{m,\mathbf{K}}
discussed in Section
6.3
, the only difference, resulting from the change of variables
(\xi,\eta)\mapsto(\eta^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}-\eta^{\prime})
, is in the multipliers
\left\langle\xi^{\prime}\right\rangle
and
\left\langle\eta^{\prime}\right\rangle
. Consequently, the bound for (
6.41
) can be obtained in the same manner. We omit the details.

7.
Proof of weighted energy estimates

In this section, we prove Proposition
5.3
. At first we consider (
5.3
). By Plancherel’s theorem, we have

\displaystyle\|P_{k}(x_{j}\phi_{\theta}(t))\|_{H^{1}}\sim\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle\|\rho_{k}(\xi)\partial_{\xi_{j}}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}.

Using (
2.15
), we see that

\displaystyle e^{-\theta it\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}\left\langle\xi\right\rangle\partial_{\xi_{j}}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}}(t,\xi)=-\theta\widehat{{\Gamma}_{j}\psi_{\theta}}(t,\xi)+\theta\partial_{\xi_{j}}\left[\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\widehat{A_{\mu}{\alpha}^{\mu}\psi}(t,\xi)\right]-e^{-\theta it\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}\frac{\xi_{j}}{\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}}(t,\xi).

This gives us that

\displaystyle\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle\|\rho_{k}(\xi)\partial_{\xi_{j}}\widehat{\phi_{\theta,\mathcal{L}}}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|P_{k}{\Gamma}_{j}\psi_{\theta,\mathcal{L}}\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|\rho_{k}(\xi)\partial_{\xi_{j}}\left[\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\widehat{A_{\mu}{\alpha}^{\mu}\psi}(t,\xi)\right]\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}+\left\|P_{k}\phi_{\theta,\mathcal{L}}\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{H(n+1){\delta}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(n+1)}.

For this we used Proposition
5.2
and (
4.9
).

Let us move on to the proof of (
5.4
). By (
2.17
), we obtain

\displaystyle e^{\theta it|\xi|}|\xi|\partial_{\xi_{j}}\widehat{V_{\mu,\mathcal{L},\theta}}(t,\xi)=-\theta\widehat{{\Gamma}_{j}W_{\mu,\mathcal{L},\theta}}(t,\xi)+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\xi_{j}}\left[|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\mathcal{L}\left\langle\psi,{\alpha}_{\mu}\psi\right\rangle}(\xi)\right]-e^{\theta it|\xi|}\frac{\xi_{j}}{|\xi|}\widehat{V_{\mu,\mathcal{L},\theta}}(t,\xi).

Then, Proposition
5.2
and (
4.24
) yield that

\displaystyle 2^{k}\|\rho_{k}(\xi)\partial_{\xi_{j}}\widehat{V_{\mu,\mathcal{L},\theta}}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|P_{k}{\Gamma}_{j}W_{\mu,\mathcal{L},\theta}\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|\rho_{k}(\xi)\partial_{\xi_{j}}\left[|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\mathcal{L}\left\langle\psi,{\alpha}_{\mu}\psi\right\rangle}(\xi)\right]\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}+\left\|P_{k}W_{\mu,\mathcal{L},\theta}\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{H(n+1){\delta}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(n+1)}.

This completes the proof of (
5.4
).

8.
Asymptotic behavior for the spinor field

This section is devoted to proving the bound of scattering norm and the modified scattering for spinor fields under the a priori assumptions (
3.3
)–(
3.5
) on
[0,T]
. In view of the definition of scattering norm (
3.1
), we need to show the following estimates

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left\|\widehat{P_{k}\phi_{\theta}}(t)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}&\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20},\\
\left\|\widehat{P_{k}\phi_{\theta}}(t)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}&\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-\frac{{\delta}}{2}m}2^{k+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-38}\end{aligned}

(8.1)

for
t\in[2^{m}-2,2^{m+1}]\cap[0,T]
. This gives us the bound (
5.5
). Now we define the phase modification symbol by

\displaystyle B_{\theta}(t,\xi)

\displaystyle=\Pi_{\theta}(\xi){\alpha}^{\mu}\int_{0}^{t}P_{\leq K}A_{\mu}\left(s,\frac{\theta s\xi}{\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}\right)ds,

(8.2)

where
K=K(s)
is an integer satisfying
2^{K}\leq\left\langle s\right\rangle^{-\frac{2}{3}-10H(2){\delta}}<2^{K+1}
and
\theta\in\{+,-\}
. We also define a profile associated with scattering by

\displaystyle\Psi_{\theta}(t,\xi):=e^{-iB_{\theta}(t,\xi)}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}}(t,\xi)=e^{-iB_{\theta}(t,\xi)}e^{\theta it\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}\widehat{\psi_{\theta}}(t,\xi).

Then the estimates of (
8.1
) are equivalent to the bounds

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(t_{2},\xi)-\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(t_{1},\xi)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t_{2}\right\rangle^{-{\delta}}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20},

(8.3)

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(t_{2},\xi)-\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(t_{1},\xi)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t_{2}\right\rangle^{-\frac{{\delta}}{2}}2^{k+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-38},

(8.4)

for
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}
and
t_{1}\leq t_{2}\in[0,T]
. Let
t_{1}\leq t_{2}\in[2^{m}-2,2^{m+1}]\cap[0,T]
. Once (
8.3
) is established, the proof of (
8.4
) is quite straightforward. More precisely, the bound (
8.4
) follows from (
3.15
) if
2^{k}\geq 2^{\frac{m}{40}{\delta}}
. On the other hand, the estimate (
8.3
) implies (
8.4
) directly if
2^{k}\leq 2^{\frac{m}{40}{\delta}}
.

For (
8.3
) let us observe that

\displaystyle\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(t_{2},\xi)-\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(t_{1},\xi)=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\partial_{s}\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(s,\xi)ds

(8.5)

and

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(s,\xi)=e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\left(\partial_{s}\widehat{P_{k}\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi)-i\left[\partial_{s}B_{\theta}(s,\xi)\right]\widehat{P_{k}\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi)\right).

(8.6)

The phase modification (
8.2
) plays a role of subtracting the resonance interaction in (
8.6
). Indeed, the explicit form can be derived to track the resonance case of nonlinearity of Dirac part. Taking Fourier transform, we see that

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi)=c\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\sum_{{\theta_{1}}\in\{\pm\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{is\left(\theta\left\langle\xi\right\rangle-{\theta_{1}}\left\langle\xi-\eta\right\rangle\right)}\widehat{A_{\mu}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{\theta_{1}}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta,

(8.7)

where
c=\frac{i}{(2\pi)^{3}}
.
In view of (
2.6
), since the resonance does not occur when
\theta\neq{\theta_{1}}
, we take into account the case
\theta={\theta_{1}}
. We simply observe that the phase interaction satisfies

\displaystyle\theta\left\langle\xi\right\rangle-\theta\left\langle\xi-\eta\right\rangle=\theta\frac{\xi\eta}{\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}+O(|\eta|^{2}).

(8.8)

Using (
8.8
), we can rewrite (
8.7
) and hence (
8.5
) as follows:

\displaystyle\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(t_{2},\xi)-\widehat{P_{k}\Psi_{\theta}}(t_{1},\xi)=

\displaystyle\quad c\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\rho_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{\theta is\frac{\xi\eta}{\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}}\rho_{\leq K}(\eta)\widehat{A_{\mu}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\left(\widehat{\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)-\widehat{\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi)\right)\,d\eta ds

(8.9)

\displaystyle+c\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\rho_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left(e^{\theta is(\left\langle\xi\right\rangle-\left\langle\xi-\eta\right\rangle)}-e^{\theta is\frac{\xi\eta}{\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}}\right)\rho_{\leq K}(\eta)\widehat{A_{\mu}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds

(8.10)

\displaystyle+c\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\rho_{k}(\xi)\sum_{{\theta_{1}}\in\{\pm\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{\theta is\left(\left\langle\xi\right\rangle-\left\langle\xi-\eta\right\rangle\right)}\rho_{>K}(\eta)\widehat{A_{\mu}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{\theta_{1}}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds

(8.11)

\displaystyle+c\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\rho_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{\theta is\left(\left\langle\xi\right\rangle+\left\langle\xi-\eta\right\rangle\right)}\rho_{\leq K}(\eta)\widehat{A_{\mu}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{-\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds.

(8.12)

Note that
2^{K}\sim 2^{-\frac{2}{3}m-10H(2){\delta}m}
.

We begin with the proof of (
8.1
) and (
8.3
) with the dyadic decomposition into low, high, and mid frequencies. For the low and high frequencies, we estimate (
8.1
) directly (See Section
8.1
below). For the mid frequency part, we prove (
8.3
) by handling the resonance decomposition (
8.9
)–(
8.12
) (See Section
8.2
below).

8.1.
Proof for the high and low frequencies.

We prove (
8.1
) for the frequency range:

\displaystyle 2^{k}\leq 2^{-30H(2){\delta}m}\;\;\mbox{ or }\;\;2^{k}\geq 2^{2H(2){\delta}m}.

By (
3.8
), we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{P_{k}\phi_{\theta}}(t)\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{-\frac{3k}{2}}\left(\sup_{j\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}\|Q_{jk}\phi_{\theta}\|_{H_{\Omega}^{0,1}}\right)^{\frac{1-\zeta}{2}}\left(\sup_{j\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}2^{j+k}\|Q_{jk}\phi_{\theta}\|_{H_{\Omega}^{0,1}}\right)^{\frac{1+\zeta}{2}}

for any
\zeta\in(0,1)
.
From (
3.17
), it follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{j\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}2^{j}\|Q_{jk}\phi_{\theta,\mathcal{L}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{H(n+1){\delta}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(n+1)-1}

(8.13)

for
\mathcal{L}\in\mathcal{V}_{n}\,(0\leq n\leq 2)
, which implies

\displaystyle\|\widehat{P_{k}\phi_{\theta}}(t)\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(2)-1}.

(8.14)

For the high frequencies
2^{k}\geq 2^{2H(2){\delta}m}
, the bounds (
8.14
) and (
5.1
) yield the desired bound (
8.1
).

Let us move on to the low frequency regime
2^{k}\leq 2^{-30H(2){\delta}m}
. This can be done by obtaining

\displaystyle\|P_{k}\phi_{\theta,{\Omega}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{2H(2){\delta}m}2^{k+\frac{k}{6}}.

(8.15)

Note that the above estimate is an improvement of (
8.13
) by the factor
2^{\frac{k}{6}}
. Then (
3.13
) yields that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{P_{k}\phi_{\theta}}(t)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{2H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{1-\zeta}{12}k}.

This finishes the proof of (
8.1
) for
2^{k}\leq 2^{-30H(2){\delta}m}
.

We now prove (
8.15
). By (
3.14
), it suffices to show

\displaystyle\|P_{k}\phi_{\theta,{\Omega}}\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{l=1}^{3}\left\|\rho_{k}(\xi)\widehat{x_{l}\phi_{\theta,{\Omega}}}(t,\xi)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2H(2){\delta}}2^{\frac{k}{6}}.

Since the first term in the left-hand side can be handled by (
3.15
) straightforwardly, we focus on the second term. Using (
2.15
), we prove

\displaystyle\|P_{k}{\Gamma}_{l}\psi_{\theta,{\Omega}}\|_{L^{2}}+\left\|P_{k}(x_{l}\mathfrak{N}_{\Omega}^{\mathbf{D}})(t)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2H(2){\delta}}2^{\frac{k}{6}}.

The estimates for the first term also is obtained by Proposition
5.2
. Thus, we consider

\displaystyle\left\|P_{k}\mathfrak{N}_{\Omega}^{\mathbf{D}}(t)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2H(2){\delta}}2^{\frac{k}{6}}\min(\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-1},2^{k}),

which implies

\displaystyle\left\|P_{k}(x_{l}\mathfrak{N}_{\Omega}^{\mathbf{D}})(t)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2\widetilde{H}(2){\delta}}2^{\frac{k}{6}}.

Let
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}
satisfy
2^{k}\leq 2^{-30H(2){\delta}m}
. We prove

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\|P_{k}\left(P_{k_{2}}A_{\mu,{\theta_{2}},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t){\alpha}^{\mu}{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t)\right)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2H(2){\delta}}2^{\frac{k}{6}}\min(\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-1},2^{k}),

(8.16)

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\|P_{k}x_{l}\left(P_{k_{2}}A_{\mu,{\theta_{2}},\mathcal{L}_{2}}(t){\alpha}^{\mu}{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t)\right)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2H(2){\delta}}2^{\frac{k}{6}}

(8.17)

for
l=1,2,3
,
\mathcal{L}_{\ell}\in\mathcal{V}_{n_{\ell}}(\ell=1,2)
, and
n_{1}+n_{2}\leq 1
.
To show (
8.16
), we utilize the estimates in the proof of Lemma
4.1
. We first consider the cases:
2^{k}\leq\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-1}
and
\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-1}\leq 2^{k}\leq 2^{-{\delta}m}
. By (
3.15
) and (
3.18
), we have

\displaystyle\|P_{k}(P_{k_{2}}A_{\mu,\mathcal{L}_{2},{\theta_{2}}}(t)

\displaystyle{\alpha}^{\mu}{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t))\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-k_{2}}{2}}\|{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}W_{\mu,\mathcal{L}_{2},{\theta_{2}}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2H(2){\delta}}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-k_{2}}{2}}2^{k_{1}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(n_{1})}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(n_{2})}.

This estimate shows (
8.16
) when
2^{k}\leq\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-1}
.

If
\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-1}\leq 2^{k}\leq 2^{-30H(2){\delta}m}
, we use (
3.18
) and (
4.4
) to obtain

\displaystyle\|P_{k}(

\displaystyle P_{k_{2}}A_{\mu,\mathcal{L}_{2},{\theta_{2}}}(t){\alpha}^{\mu}{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t))\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-\frac{k_{2}}{2}}\|\psi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\|W_{\mu,\mathcal{L}_{2},{\theta_{2}}}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{\left[H(n_{1}+1)+H(n_{2}+1)\right]{\delta}+\frac{{\delta}}{2}}2^{k_{1}}\min(\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-1},2^{k_{2}})\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(n_{1}+1)+1}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(n_{2}+1)+2}.

The bound (
8.16
) follows from the summation over
k,k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}
apart from the case
2^{k}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{2}}
and
2^{k_{1}}\geq 2^{\frac{k}{3}}
. For the remaining case, we divide
{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}=\phi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}^{\leq J,k_{1}}+\phi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}^{>J,k_{1}}
with
2^{J}=C\left\langle t\right\rangle 2^{k_{1}}
for some
C\ll 1
. Then, (
3.21
) and (
3.17
) yield that

\displaystyle\|e^{-{\theta_{1}}it\left\langle D\right\rangle}\phi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}^{\leq J,k_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{H(n_{1}+2)-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{{\delta}}{2}}2^{-\frac{k_{1}}{2}},

\displaystyle\|\phi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}^{>J,k_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{H(n_{1}+1)-1}2^{-k_{1}}.

By these we have

\displaystyle\|P_{k}(P_{k_{2}}A_{\mu,\mathcal{L}_{2},{\theta_{2}}}(t)

\displaystyle{\alpha}^{\mu}e^{-{\theta_{1}}it\left\langle D\right\rangle}\phi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}^{\leq J,k_{1}}(t))\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-\frac{k_{2}}{2}}\|e^{-{\theta_{1}}it\left\langle D\right\rangle}\phi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}^{\leq J,k_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|W_{\mu,\mathcal{L}_{2},{\theta_{2}}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{2H(2){\delta}-\frac{3}{2}}2^{-\frac{k_{1}+k_{2}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(n_{2})}

and

\displaystyle\|P_{k}(P_{k_{2}}A_{\mu,\mathcal{L}_{2},{\theta_{2}}}(t)

\displaystyle{\alpha}^{\mu}e^{-{\theta_{1}}it\left\langle D\right\rangle}\phi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}^{>J,k_{1}}(t))\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{3k-k_{2}}{2}}\|\phi_{{\theta_{1}},\mathcal{L}_{1}}^{>J,k_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\|W_{\mu,\mathcal{L}_{2},{\theta_{2}}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{\left[H(n_{1}+1)+H(n_{2})\right]{\delta}-1}2^{\frac{3k-2k_{1}-k_{2}}{2}}.

We get the bound (
8.16
) for
2^{k}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{2}}
and
2^{k_{1}}\geq 2^{\frac{k}{3}}
.
Since the proof of (
8.17
) is similar to that of Lemma
4.2
, we omit the details.

8.2.
Proof for mid frequencies

This section is devoted to taking on the mid frequency regime

\displaystyle 2^{-30H(2){\delta}m}\leq 2^{k}\leq 2^{2H(2){\delta}m}.

(8.18)

As observed in (
8.7
), we estimate (
8.1
) on the regime (
8.18
) by decomposition (
8.9
)–(
8.12
).

Bound for (
8.9
).

By the frequency localization, we see that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \eqref{eq:correction-1}&=c\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\rho_{k}(\xi)e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{\theta is\frac{\xi\eta}{\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}}\rho_{\leq K}(\eta)|\eta|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}W_{\mu}}(s,\eta)\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times{\alpha}^{\mu}\left[\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)-\widehat{P_{k_{1}}\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi)\right]d\eta ds.\end{aligned}

By the frequency cut-off function with respect to
\xi
and
\eta
, it suffices to consider the case
2^{k_{2}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k}
. To investigate the difference between spinors in the integrand, we make a further decomposition
{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}=\sum_{j_{1}\in\mathcal{U}_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}^{j_{1},k_{1}}
. By (
3.7
) and (
3.17
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{j_{1},k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{j_{1}}{2}+\frac{{\delta}j_{1}}{8}}2^{-{k_{1}}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}.

(8.19)

Using this estimate and mean value theorem, we see from (
3.6
) that

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{j_{1},k_{1}}}(s,\xi-\eta)-\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{j_{1},k_{1}}}(s,\xi)\right|\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{H(2){\delta}m}2^{k_{2}}2^{\frac{j_{1}}{2}+\frac{{\delta}j_{1}}{8}}2^{-{k_{1}}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}.

(8.20)

Let
J_{0}
be an integer such that
2^{J_{0}}\sim 2^{\frac{2}{3}m+20H(2){\delta}m}
. Then the bounds (
8.19
) and (
8.20
) imply that

\displaystyle\sum_{j_{1}>J_{0}}\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{j_{1},k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{J_{0}}{2}+\frac{{\delta}J_{0}}{8}}2^{-{k_{1}}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)},

\displaystyle\sum_{j_{1}\leq J_{0}}\left|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{j_{1},k_{1}}}(s,\xi-\eta)-\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{j_{1},k_{1}}}(s,\xi)\right|\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{H(2){\delta}m}2^{k_{2}}2^{\frac{J_{0}}{2}+\frac{{\delta}J_{0}}{8}}2^{-{k_{1}}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)},

and hence we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\eqref{eq:correction-1}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2^{k_{2}}\leq 2^{K},2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k}}{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{m}2^{H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k_{2}}{2}}2^{-\frac{J_{0}}{2}+\frac{{\delta}J_{0}}{8}}2^{-{k_{1}}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\|\rho_{k_{2}}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}W_{\mu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{2^{k_{2}}\leq 2^{K},2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k}}{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{m}2^{H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{k_{2}}{2}}2^{\frac{J_{0}}{2}+\frac{{\delta}J_{0}}{8}}2^{-\frac{k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\|\rho_{\leq K}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}W_{\mu}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{m}2^{[16H(2)+1]{\delta}m}2^{K}2^{-\frac{J_{0}}{2}}2^{-\frac{k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{m}2^{[H(2)+1]{\delta}m}2^{2K}2^{\frac{J_{0}}{2}}2^{2H(0){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}.

Since
2^{K}\sim 2^{m(-\frac{2}{3}+10H(2){\delta})}
,
2^{J_{0}}\sim 2^{\frac{2}{3}m+20H(2){\delta}m}
, using the restriction (
8.18
) and frequency relation
2^{k}\sim 2^{k_{1}}
, we get the first part of (
8.1
).

Bound for (
8.10
).

(
8.10
) can be treated similarly to (
8.9
). By dyadic decomposition, one gets

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\rho_{k}(\xi)e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left[e^{\theta is(\left\langle\xi\right\rangle-\left\langle\xi-\eta\right\rangle)}-e^{\theta is\frac{\xi\eta}{\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}}\right]\rho_{\leq K}(\eta)\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}A_{\mu}}(s,\eta)\\
&\hskip 227.62204pt\times{\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds\end{aligned}

(8.21)

for
k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}
. As we observed in the proof of bound for (
8.9
), we have only to consider the case
2^{k_{2}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k}
. Using (
8.8
) and (
8.19
), we see that

\displaystyle\sum_{2^{k_{2}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}}\left|\eqref{eq:aim-cor-2}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2^{k_{2}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}}2^{2m}2^{3K}\|{P_{k_{2}}}W_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2^{k_{2}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}}{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{[1+H(2)]{\delta}m-30H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{1}{100}k}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}.

Bound for (
8.11
).
Analogously, we consider the following integrand

\displaystyle c\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)\rho_{k}(\xi)e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{\theta isp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}\rho_{>K}(\eta)|\eta|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta_{1}}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds

(8.22)

for
k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}
. For this we exploit the space-time resonances (
2.6
) and (
2.7
) suitably. In view of the phase interaction (
2.6
), the most delicate part is the resonance case
\theta={\theta_{1}}
. We can deal with the other case similarly but with a better bound and hence we will omit it. For the case
\theta={\theta_{1}}
we use the integration by parts in time and see that (
8.22
) is the linear combination of the following:

\displaystyle e^{-iB_{\theta}(t,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{itp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}M_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta)\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(t,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}}(t,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds,\quad(t=t_{1},t_{2}),

(8.23)

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\partial_{s}B_{\theta}(s,\xi)e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{isp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}M_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta)\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds,

(8.24)

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{isp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}M_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta)\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}\partial_{s}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds,

(8.25)

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{isp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}M_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta)\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\partial_{s}\phi_{\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds,

(8.26)

where

\displaystyle M_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta):=\frac{\Pi_{\theta}(\xi)|\eta|^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{p_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}\rho_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta)\rho_{>K}(\eta)\;\;\mbox{and}\;\;\rho_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta)=\rho_{k}(\xi)\widetilde{\rho}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\widetilde{\rho}_{k_{2}}(\eta).

Then we have

\displaystyle\left|M_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta)\right|\lesssim 2^{-\frac{3k_{2}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle\left\langle 2^{\max(\mathbf{k})}\right\rangle\lesssim 2^{-\frac{3k_{2}}{2}}2^{4H(2){\delta}m}.

Let us handle (
8.23
). To this end, we make a decomposition
{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}=\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}+\phi_{\theta}^{>J,k_{1}}
with
2^{J}=2^{m}
. Then,
by (
8.19
), one obtains

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{>J,k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{H(2){\delta}m-\frac{1}{2}m}2^{-k_{1}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}.

(8.27)

This leads us to the estimate

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}|\eqref{eq:cor-3-1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{4H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-3k_{2}}{2}}\left\|\phi_{\theta}^{>J,k_{1}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{[H(0)+5H(2)]{\delta}m-\frac{m}{2}}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-3k_{2}}{2}-k_{1}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-\frac{m}{4}}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

Note that
2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-3k_{2}}{2}-k_{1}}\lesssim 2^{30H(2){\delta}m}
from the frequency relation and (
8.18
). On the other hand, for
\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}
, we exploit the space resonance (
2.7
). Indeed, we integrate by parts in
\eta
. Then, (
8.23
) equipped with
\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}
is the linear combination of the following:

\displaystyle t^{-1}e^{-iB_{\theta}(t,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{itp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}\nabla_{\eta}M_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta)\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(t,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(t,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds,

(8.28)

\displaystyle t^{-1}e^{-iB_{\theta}(t,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{itp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}M_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta)\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}xV_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(t,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds,

(8.29)

\displaystyle t^{-1}e^{-iB_{\theta}(t,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{itp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}M_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta)\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(t,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{x\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds,

(8.30)

where
t=t_{1},\,t_{2}
and

\displaystyle M_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta)=\frac{\nabla_{\eta}p_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}p_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}M_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta).

A direct calculation gives us the bound

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{\eta}^{\ell}M^{1}_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta)\right|\lesssim 2^{-\frac{3k_{2}}{2}-\ell k_{2}}2^{2(4+2\ell)H(2){\delta}m}

for
\ell=0,1
. By (
8.19
), one readily gets

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}.

(8.31)

Then, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\left|\eqref{eq:cor-3-11}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{-m+12H(2){\delta}m}2^{-k_{2}}\|P_{k_{2}}W_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-\frac{m}{6}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

The estimates for (
8.29
) can be done similarly by using (
3.16
). In view of (
8.19
) and (
3.6
), one can see that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{x\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim\sum_{j\leq J}{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{j_{1}}{2}}2^{-k_{1}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{H(2){\delta}m+\frac{1}{2}m}2^{-k_{1}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}.

(8.32)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\left|\eqref{eq:cor-3-13}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{-m}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{4}\|P_{k_{2}}W_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\widehat{x\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{[H(0)+9H(2)]{\delta}m-\frac{m}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-\frac{m}{4}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

Therefore, we have the desired bounds for (
8.28
)–(
8.30
).

Let us move on to estimates for (
8.24
). From the definition of
B_{\theta}(s,\xi)
in (
8.2
) and (
3.20
), we have

\displaystyle|\partial_{s}B_{\theta}(s,\xi)|\lesssim\left|P_{\leq K}A_{\mu}\left(s,\frac{s\xi}{\left\langle\xi\right\rangle}\right)\right|\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{11{\delta}m-m}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+2}.

(8.33)

By the proof for (
8.23
) this finishes the proof for (
8.24
).

To estimate (
8.25
), we use decomposition
{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta}=\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}+\phi_{\theta}^{>J,k_{1}}
with
2^{J}=2^{m-60H(2){\delta}m}
and have

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{>J,k_{1}}}\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{31H(2){\delta}m-\frac{1}{2}m}2^{-k_{1}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}.

Then, by (
4.16
), one gets

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}|\eqref{eq:cor-3-3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{m}2^{4H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-3k_{2}}{2}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}\partial_{s}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{>J,k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{37H(2){\delta}m-\frac{1}{2}m}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-3k_{2}}{2}-k_{1}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)+5}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-\frac{m}{4}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-25}.

We now consider
\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}
in (
8.25
). For this case, we can obtain an extra time decay by exploiting the space resonance (
2.7
). Indeed, we write (
8.25
) with
\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}
as the linear combination of the following:

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}s^{-1}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{isp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}\nabla_{\eta}M_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{s}\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds,

(8.34)

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}s^{-1}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{isp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}M_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{s}\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}xV_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds,

(8.35)

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}s^{-1}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{isp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}M_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{s}\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{x\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s,\xi-\eta)\,d\eta ds.

(8.36)

By Hölder’s inequality and (
8.19
), we estimate

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\left|\eqref{eq:cor-3-31}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{12H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-5k_{2}}{2}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}\partial_{s}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{-m+14H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-5k_{2}-k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)+5}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-\frac{m}{6}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

Note that
2^{k_{2}}\gtrsim 2^{K}\sim 2^{-\frac{2}{3}m-10H(2){\delta}m}
. Regarding (
8.35
), we use Lemma
3.10
to obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\left|\eqref{eq:cor-3-32}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{8H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-3k_{2}}{2}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}\partial_{s}(xV_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s))\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{-\frac{m}{8}+9H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-3k_{2}-k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)+5}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-\frac{m}{10}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

Using the similar bound to (
8.32
), we see that

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\left|\eqref{eq:cor-3-33}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{8H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-3k_{2}}{2}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}\partial_{s}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\widehat{x\phi_{\theta}^{\leq J,k_{1}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{-50H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{3\min(\mathbf{k})-3k_{2}}{2}-k_{1}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)+5}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

It remains to handle (
8.26
). Note that the frequency relation has the trichotomy:
2^{k}\lesssim 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{2}}
,
2^{k_{1}}\ll 2^{k}\sim 2^{k_{1}}
, and
2^{k_{2}}\ll 2^{k}\sim 2^{k_{1}}
. As observed previously, the last case becomes the most difficult case. Hence, we consider only the case
2^{k_{2}}\ll 2^{k}\sim 2^{k_{1}}
. By direct calculation and dyadic decomposition, (
8.26
) can be written as the linear combination of the following:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3+3}}e^{isr_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta)\widehat{{P_{k_{4}}}V_{\nu,{\theta_{4}}}}(s,\sigma)\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times{\alpha}^{\mu}{\alpha}^{\nu}\widehat{\phi_{{\theta_{3}}}^{>J,k_{3}}}(s,\xi-\eta-\sigma)\,d\sigma d\eta ds,\end{aligned}

(8.37)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\sum_{a,b=1,2}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3+3}}e^{isr_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\widehat{V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}^{a}}(s,\eta)\widehat{V_{\nu,{\theta_{4}}}^{b}}(s,\sigma)\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times{\alpha}^{\mu}{\alpha}^{\nu}\widehat{\phi_{{\theta_{3}}}^{\leq J,k_{3}}}(s,\xi-\eta-\sigma)\,d\sigma d\eta ds,\end{aligned}

(8.38)

where
2^{J}=2^{m}
,

\displaystyle r_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)

\displaystyle=\theta\left\langle\xi\right\rangle+{\theta_{2}}|\eta|-{\theta_{3}}\left\langle\xi-\eta-\sigma\right\rangle+{\theta_{4}}|\sigma|,

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)

\displaystyle=M_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta)|\sigma|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rho_{k_{3}}(\xi-\eta-\sigma)\rho_{k_{4}}(\sigma),

and
V_{{\lambda},{\kappa}}^{1}:=V_{{\lambda},{\kappa}}^{>J,k}
,
V_{{\lambda},{\kappa}}^{2}:=V_{{\lambda},{\kappa}}^{\leq J,k}
for
{\lambda}\in\{\mu,\nu\}
and
{\kappa}\in\{{\theta_{2}},{\theta_{4}}\}
. Note that the frequency relation can be divided into
2^{k_{1}}\lesssim 2^{k_{3}}\sim 2^{k_{4}}
,
2^{k_{3}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{4}}
, and
2^{k_{4}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{3}}
and the multiplier has the bound

\displaystyle\left|\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\right|\lesssim 2^{4H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{3k_{2}+k_{4}}{2}}.

We first consider (
8.37
). If
2^{k_{4}}\geq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}
, (
3.20
), (
3.17
), and (
3.15
) yield that

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2},k_{3}\in{\mathbb{Z}}\\
2^{k_{4}}\geq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle\left|\eqref{eq:cor-3-41}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2},k_{3}\in{\mathbb{Z}}\\
2^{k_{4}}\geq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\end{subarray}}2^{m+4H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{3k_{2}+k_{4}}{2}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}W_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\phi_{\theta_{3}}^{>J,k_{3}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|{P_{k_{4}}}W_{\mu,{\theta_{4}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2},k_{3}\in{\mathbb{Z}}\\
2^{k_{4}}\geq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\end{subarray}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-m+7H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{2k_{2}+k_{4}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+2}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}\left\langle 2^{k_{4}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

On the other hand, if
2^{k_{4}}\leq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}
,
from the fact that
2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k}\in[2^{-30H(2){\delta}m},2^{2H(2){\delta}m}]
, only the case
2^{k_{4}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{3}}
appears. Hence by (
8.27
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2^{k_{4}}\leq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\\
2^{k_{4}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{3}}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle\left|\eqref{eq:cor-3-41}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2^{k_{4}}\leq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\\
2^{k_{4}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{3}}\end{subarray}}2^{m+4H(2){\delta}m}2^{k_{4}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}W_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\widehat{\phi_{\theta_{3}}^{>J,k_{3}}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\|{P_{k_{4}}}W_{\mu,{\theta_{4}}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2^{k_{4}}\leq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\\
2^{k_{4}}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{3}}\end{subarray}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{\frac{m}{2}+7H(2){\delta}m}2^{k_{4}}2^{-\frac{k_{3}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\left\langle 2^{k_{4}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

In the last inequality, we used the condition
2^{-30H(2){\delta}m}\lesssim 2^{k_{3}}
and
2^{k}\sim 2^{k_{3}}
.

We consider (
8.38
) with
(a,b)=(1,1)
. If
2^{k_{3}}\geq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}
, by (
3.17
), (
8.31
), and Hölder’s inequality, we estimate

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2},k_{4}\in{\mathbb{Z}}\\
2^{k_{3}}\geq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\end{subarray}}

\displaystyle\left|\eqref{eq:cor-3-42}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2},k_{4}\in{\mathbb{Z}}\\
2^{k_{3}}\geq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\end{subarray}}2^{m+4H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k_{4}}{2}}\|\rho_{k_{4}}\|_{L_{\sigma}^{2}}\|V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}^{>J,k_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|\phi_{\theta_{3}}^{\leq J,k_{3}}(s)\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\|{P_{k_{4}}}V_{\mu,{\theta_{4}}}^{>J,k_{4}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{2},k_{4}\in{\mathbb{Z}}\\
2^{k_{3}}\geq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\end{subarray}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-m+7H(2){\delta}m}2^{-k_{2}-\frac{k_{3}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}\left\langle 2^{k_{4}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

If
2^{k_{3}}\leq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}
, then the frequency relation gives us only the case
2^{k_{3}}\ll 2^{k_{4}}\sim 2^{k_{1}}\in[2^{-30H(2){\delta}m},2^{2H(2){\delta}m}]
. Then, by (
3.20
), (
3.17
), and the above estimate done with
\|\rho_{k_{3}}\|_{L_{\sigma}^{2}}
instead of
\|\rho_{k_{4}}\|_{L_{\sigma}^{2}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2^{k_{3}}\leq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\\
2^{k_{3}}\ll 2^{k_{4}}\sim 2^{k_{1}}\end{subarray}}\left|\eqref{eq:cor-3-42}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2^{k_{3}}\leq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\\
2^{k_{3}}\ll 2^{k_{4}}\sim 2^{k_{1}}\end{subarray}}2^{m+4H(2){\delta}m}2^{\frac{3k_{3}-k_{4}}{2}}\left\|V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}^{>J,k_{2}}(s)\right\|_{L^{2}}\left\|\phi_{\theta_{3}}^{\leq J,k_{3}}(s)\right\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\left\|V_{\mu,{\theta_{4}}}^{>J,k_{4}}(s)\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2^{k_{3}}\leq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}\\
2^{k_{3}}\ll 2^{k_{4}}\sim 2^{k_{1}}\end{subarray}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-m+7H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{2k_{2}+3k_{4}}{2}}2^{k_{3}}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)}\left\langle 2^{k_{4}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-20}.

For the estimate of (
8.38
) with
(a,b)=(2,1)
. Analogously to (
2.7
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{\eta}r_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\right|\geq\left\langle\xi-\eta-\sigma\right\rangle^{-2}.

Keeping this lower bound in mind, we integrate by parts in
\eta
and write (
8.38
) as the linear combination of the following:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}s^{-1}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3+3}}e^{isr_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}\nabla_{\eta}\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\widehat{V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}^{\leq J,k_{2}}}(s,\eta)\widehat{V_{\nu,{\theta_{4}}}^{>J,k_{4}}}(\sigma)\\
&\hskip 227.62204pt\times{\alpha}^{\mu}{\alpha}^{\nu}\widehat{\phi_{{\theta_{3}}}^{\leq J,k_{3}}}(s,\xi-\eta-\sigma)\,d\sigma d\eta ds,\end{aligned}

(8.39)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}s^{-1}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3+3}}e^{isr_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\widehat{xV_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}^{\leq J,k_{2}}}(s,\eta)\widehat{V_{\nu,{\theta_{4}}}^{>J,k_{4}}}(\sigma)\\
&\hskip 227.62204pt\times{\alpha}^{\mu}{\alpha}^{\nu}\widehat{\phi_{{\theta_{3}}}^{\leq J,k_{3}}}(s,\xi-\eta-\sigma)\,d\sigma d\eta ds,\end{aligned}

(8.40)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}s^{-1}e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3+3}}e^{isr_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\widehat{V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}^{\leq J,k_{2}}}(s,\eta)\widehat{V_{\nu,{\theta_{4}}}^{>J,k_{4}}}(\sigma)\\
&\hskip 227.62204pt\times{\alpha}^{\mu}{\alpha}^{\nu}\widehat{x\phi_{{\theta_{3}}}^{\leq J,k_{3}}}(s,\xi-\eta-\sigma)\,d\sigma d\eta ds,\end{aligned}

(8.41)

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)=\frac{\nabla_{\eta}r_{\theta}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)}{|\nabla_{\eta}r_{\theta}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)|^{2}}\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}(\xi,\eta,\sigma).

The multiplier is bounded by

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{\eta}^{\ell}\widetilde{M}_{\mathbf{k}}^{1}(\xi,\eta,\sigma)\right|\lesssim 2^{4H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{3k_{2}}{2}-\ell k_{2}}2^{-\frac{k_{4}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{2+2\ell}\;\;(\ell=0,1).

Then we estimate

\displaystyle|\eqref{eq:cor-3-42-eta-1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{4H(2){\delta}m}2^{-k_{2}}2^{k_{4}}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{4}\|V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}^{\leq J,k_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|\phi_{\theta_{3}}^{\leq J,k_{3}}(s)\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\|V_{\mu,{\theta_{4}}}^{>J,k_{4}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-m+20H(2){\delta}m}2^{-k_{2}}2^{-\frac{k_{3}+k_{4}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)+4}\left\langle 2^{k_{4}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}.

Summing both sides over
2^{\min(k_{3},k_{4})}\geq 2^{-\frac{7}{12}m}
, we get the desired bound. On the other hand, since

\displaystyle\|V_{\mu,{\theta_{4}}}^{>J,k_{4}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{-rm}2^{-rk_{4}}\left\langle 2^{k_{4}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}

for
0\leq r\leq 1
by (
3.17
), it follows that, for
\frac{1}{4}<r<1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}|\eqref{eq:cor-3-42-eta-1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{4H(2){\delta}m}2^{-k_{2}}2^{-\frac{k_{4}}{2}}2^{\frac{3\min(k_{3},k_{4})}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{4}\|V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}^{\leq J,k_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|\phi_{\theta_{3}}^{\leq J,k_{3}}(s)\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\|V_{\nu,{\theta_{4}}}^{>J,k_{4}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-rm+7H(2){\delta}m}2^{-k_{2}}2^{-\frac{k_{3}+k_{4}}{2}}2^{\frac{3\min(k_{3},k_{4})}{2}}2^{-rk_{4}}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)+4}\left\langle 2^{k_{4}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{20}.

Note that we summed for
\max(k_{3},k_{4})
by using the frequency relation trichotomy. The estimates for (
8.40
) and (
8.41
) are quite similar. Hence, we treat only the estimate for (
8.40
). By (
3.26
),

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}|\eqref{eq:cor-3-42-eta-2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{4H(2){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k_{4}}{2}}2^{\frac{3\min(k_{3},k_{4})}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{2}\|xV_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}^{\leq J,k_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}\|\phi_{\theta_{3}}^{\leq J,k_{3}}(s)\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\|V_{\nu,{\theta_{4}}}^{>J,k_{4}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k_{2},k_{3},k_{4}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-m+7H(2){\delta}m}2^{-k_{2}}2^{2^{\frac{3\min(k_{3},k_{4})-k_{3}-3k_{4}}{2}}}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}\left\langle 2^{k_{3}}\right\rangle^{-N(2)+2}\left\langle 2^{k_{4}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{3}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}+\frac{k}{100}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{20}.

For the estimates of (
8.38
) with
(a,b)=(1,2)
and
(2,2)
, we can obtain the desired bound by exploiting the resonance with respect to
\sigma
. We omit the details.

Bound for (
8.12
).
As for (
8.12
) the phase interaction does not exhibit the space-time resonance. Hence we can use the normal form approach as follows: The integration by parts in time shows that (
8.12
) consists of the following terms:

\displaystyle\rho_{k}(\xi)e^{-iB_{\theta}(t,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{e^{\theta itp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}}{p_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}\rho_{\leq K}(\eta)|\eta|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(t,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{-\theta}}(t,\xi-\eta)d\eta,\qquad(t=t_{1},\,t_{2})

(8.42)

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\rho_{k}(\xi)[\partial_{s}B_{\theta}(s,\xi)]e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{e^{\theta isp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}}{p_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}\rho_{\leq K}(\eta)|\eta|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{-\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)d\eta ds,

(8.43)

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\rho_{k}(\xi)e^{-iB_{\theta}(s,\xi)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{e^{\theta isp_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}}{p_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}\rho_{\leq K}(\eta)|\eta|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{s}\left[\widehat{V_{\mu,{\theta_{2}}}}(s,\eta){\alpha}^{\mu}\widehat{\phi_{-\theta}}(s,\xi-\eta)\right]d\eta ds.

(8.44)

From the restriction
|\eta|\leq 2^{-\frac{2}{3}m-10H(2){\delta}m}
, we readily close the estimates for (
8.42
)–(
8.44
). Especially, we can use (
8.33
) for (
8.43
).

9.
Asymptotic behavior for the gauge fields

In this section, we show the scattering norm bound (
5.6
) for Maxwell part under the a priori assumptions (
3.3
)–(
3.5
) on
[0,T]
for
T>1
.

According to the definition (
3.2
), the scattering norm bound (
5.6
) is equivalent to

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}2^{j}\left\|Q_{jk}V_{\mu,\theta}(t_{2})-Q_{jk}V_{\mu,\theta}(t_{2})\right\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-k-5H(2){\delta}k}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+5}

(9.1)

for
t_{1}\leq t_{2}\in[2^{m}-2,2^{m+1}]\cap[0,T]
with
m\in{\mathbb{Z}}
. Analogously to the proof in Section
8
, we prove (
9.1
) by decomposing
the case into low, high, and mid frequency regime. Before the decomposition, we consider the large spatial region as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{j\geq J}2^{j}\left\|Q_{jk}V_{\mu,\theta}(t_{2})-Q_{jk}V_{\mu,\theta}(t_{2})\right\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-k-5H(2){\delta}k}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+5},

(9.2)

where
2^{J}\sim 2^{m(1+H(2){\delta})}2^{|k|}
. For the difference in the norm of (
9.2
), one may use

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\partial_{s}Q_{jk}V_{\mu,\theta}(s)ds=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}Q_{jk}e^{-\theta is|D|}\mathfrak{N}_{\mu}^{\mathbf{M}}(s)ds.

(9.3)

Hence it suffices for (
9.2
) to prove

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}2^{-\frac{k}{2}}\left\|\rho_{j}(x)P_{k}\left\langle{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{\theta_{1}}(t),{\alpha}_{\mu}{P_{k_{2}}}\psi_{\theta_{2}}(t)\right\rangle\right\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{-(1+{\delta})m}2^{-(1+{\delta})j}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+5}.

(9.4)

For the sum over
\max(k_{1},k_{2})\geq j
, we have the bound (
9.2
) straightforwardly by (
3.15
). As for the sum over
\min(k_{1},k_{2})\leq-j
, (
3.18
) also implies the bound (
9.2
). We now assume
2^{k_{1}},2^{k_{2}}\in[2^{-j},2^{j}]
. For this case we further decompose

\displaystyle P_{k_{\ell}}\psi_{\theta_{\ell}}=\sum_{j_{\ell}\in\mathcal{U}_{k_{\ell}}}e^{-\theta_{\ell}it\left\langle D\right\rangle}\phi_{\theta_{\ell}}^{j_{\ell},k_{\ell}}\qquad\mbox{ for }\;\ell=1,\,2.

If
\min(j_{1},j_{2})\geq j(1-{\delta})
, by (
3.17
), we estimate

\displaystyle 2^{-\frac{k}{2}}\left\|\rho_{j}(x)P_{k}\left\langle{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{\theta_{1}}(t),{\alpha}_{\mu}{P_{k_{2}}}\psi_{\theta_{2}}(t)\right\rangle\right\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j_{\ell}\in\mathcal{U}_{k_{\ell}}}2^{k}\|\phi_{{\theta_{1}}}^{j_{1},k_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\|\phi_{{\theta_{2}}}^{j_{2},k_{2}}(t)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{j_{\ell}\in\mathcal{U}_{k_{\ell}}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{2H(1){\delta}m}2^{k}2^{-j_{1}-j_{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-(1+{\delta})m}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+1}.

On the other hand, if
\min(j_{1},j_{2})\leq j(1-{\delta})
, using the inverse Fourier transform, we see that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &P_{k}\left\langle{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{\theta_{1}}(t),{\alpha}_{\mu}{P_{k_{2}}}\psi_{\theta_{2}}(t)\right\rangle\\
&=C\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3+3}}e^{ix\xi}\rho_{k}(\xi)e^{itq_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}\left\langle\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{\theta_{1}}}(t,\eta),{\alpha}_{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}\psi_{\theta_{2}}}(t,\xi+\eta)\right\rangle d\xi d\eta.\end{aligned}

(9.5)

Then, using integration by parts in
\xi
repeatedly, (
9.5
) is bounded by

\displaystyle 2^{-nj}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3+3}}e^{ix\xi}\nabla_{\xi}^{n}\left[\rho_{k}(\xi)e^{itq_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}\left\langle\widehat{\psi_{\theta_{1}}^{j_{1},k_{1}}}(t,\eta),{\alpha}_{\mu}\widehat{\psi_{\theta_{2}}^{j_{2},k_{2}}}(t,\xi+\eta)\right\rangle\right]d\xi d\eta\right|.

(9.6)

Note that the derivative
\nabla_{\xi}
implies
2^{m}+2^{-k}+2^{j_{\ell}}
-growth in (
9.6
). Summing over
\min(j_{1},j_{2})\leq j(1-{\delta})
for suitable
n
, we get the desired bound (
9.4
).

We consider the contribution of sum over
j\leq J
in the sequel.

9.1.
Proof for the low and high frequency regimes

Let us restrict
|\xi|
as follows:

\displaystyle 2^{k}\leq 2^{-\frac{m}{100}}\;\;\mbox{ or }\;\;2^{k}\geq 2^{H(1){\delta}m}.

(9.7)

Then by (
3.17
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{j\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}2^{j}\left\|Q_{jk}V_{\mu,\theta}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{H(1){\delta}}2^{-k}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}.

Then, (
9.1
) follows for (
9.7
) from the fact that
H(1)<\frac{5H(2)}{100}
.

9.2.
Proof for mid frequency regime

We now consider the mid frequency part

\displaystyle 2^{-\frac{m}{100}}\leq 2^{k}\leq 2^{H(1){\delta}m}.

(9.8)

Using the time derivative expression in (
9.3
) and the profile expression, we can write (
9.1
) as

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\rho_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{isq_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\langle\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta_{1}}}(s,\eta),{\alpha}_{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}\phi_{\theta_{2}}}(s,\xi+\eta)\right\rangle d\eta ds.

(9.9)

Then, for the proof of (
9.1
) over the regime (
9.8
), it suffices to show that

\displaystyle 2^{J}\|\eqref{eq:s-maxwell-mid}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}2^{-{\delta}m}2^{-k-5H(2){\delta}k}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)-5}.

(9.10)

By the symmetry between
\psi_{\theta_{1}}
and
\psi_{\theta_{2}}
, we assume that
\min(k_{1},k_{2})=k_{1}
.

Let us handle first the case
2^{k_{1}}\leq 2^{-\frac{9}{10}m}
. Then, by (
3.24
), one gets

\displaystyle\|\eqref{eq:s-maxwell-mid}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2^{k_{1}}\leq 2^{-\frac{9}{10}m}}2^{m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}}2^{3k_{1}}\|\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta_{1}}}(s)\|_{L_{\xi}^{\infty}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}\phi_{\theta_{2}}(s)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-\frac{5}{4}m}2^{-10H(2){\delta}m+H(0){\delta}m}2^{-\frac{k}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+5}.

This gives us the desired bound. If
2^{\max(k_{1},k_{2})}\geq 2^{\frac{m}{50}}
, then we have the frequency relation
2^{k}\ll 2^{k_{1}}\sim 2^{k_{2}}
from (
9.8
). Using (
3.15
) together with the above summation taken over
2^{\max(k_{1},k_{2})}\geq 2^{\frac{m}{50}}
, we obtain (
9.10
).

Now it remains to handle the case
2^{k_{1}},2^{k_{2}}\in[2^{-\frac{9}{10}m},2^{\frac{m}{50}}]
. To do so, we exploit the space-time resonance (
2.8
) and (
2.9
). By the integration by parts in time, we have the decomposition of (
9.9
) as follows:

\displaystyle\rho_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{e^{itq_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}}{q_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\langle\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta_{1}}}(t,\eta),{\alpha}_{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}\phi_{\theta_{2}}}(t,\xi+\eta)\right\rangle d\eta,\qquad(t=t_{1},t_{2})

(9.11)

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\rho_{k}(\xi)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{e^{isq_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}}{q_{\Theta}(\xi,\eta)}|\xi|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\langle\partial_{s}\widehat{{P_{k_{1}}}\phi_{\theta_{1}}}(s,\eta),{\alpha}_{\mu}\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}\phi_{\theta_{2}}}(s,\xi+\eta)\right\rangle d\eta ds,

(9.12)

and the symmetric term with time derivative falling on
\widehat{{P_{k_{2}}}\phi_{\theta_{2}}}
. Let us treat (
9.11
) first. If
2^{k_{1}}\leq 2^{-\frac{1}{10}m}
, then we have the relation
2^{k_{1}}\ll 2^{k}\sim 2^{k_{2}}
. Then, by (
3.19
) and (
3.15
), one can obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2^{k_{1}}\leq 2^{-\frac{m}{10}}\\
2^{k_{1}}\ll 2^{k}\sim 2^{k_{2}}\end{subarray}}\|\eqref{eq:s-mid-1}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2^{k_{1}}\leq 2^{-\frac{m}{10}}\\
2^{k_{1}}\ll 2^{k}\sim 2^{k_{2}}\end{subarray}}\|{P_{k_{1}}}\psi_{\theta_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}\psi_{\theta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}2^{k_{1}}\leq 2^{-\frac{1}{10}m}\\
2^{k_{1}}\ll 2^{k}\sim 2^{k_{2}}\end{subarray}}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-m+4H(1){\delta}m+\frac{m}{200}}2^{-k}2^{\frac{k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-m+4H(1){\delta}m-\frac{9}{200}m}2^{-k}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+5}.

On the other hand, if
2^{k_{1}}\in[2^{-\frac{m}{10}},2^{\frac{m}{50}}]
, then by (
3.26
), we see that

\displaystyle\|e^{-{\theta_{1}}it\left\langle D\right\rangle}\phi_{\theta_{1}}^{\leq J_{0},k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}2^{-\frac{k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)},

\displaystyle\|\phi_{\theta_{1}}^{>J_{0},k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-1+H(1){\delta}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)-1},

where let
J_{0}
be an integer satisfying
2^{J_{0}}=c_{0}\left\langle t\right\rangle 2^{k_{1}}
for some fixed
0<c_{0}\ll 1
.
From the above bounds and the restriction
k_{1}\leq k_{2}
under the regime (
9.8
) it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{2^{k_{1}}\in[2^{-\frac{m}{10}},2^{\frac{m}{50}}]}\|\eqref{eq:s-mid-1}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2^{k_{1}}\in[2^{-\frac{m}{10}},2^{\frac{m}{50}}]}2^{-\frac{3}{2}k}2^{2H(1){\delta}m}\|e^{-\theta it\left\langle D\right\rangle}\phi_{\theta_{1}}^{\leq J_{0},k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}\psi_{\theta_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2^{k_{1}}\in[2^{-\frac{m}{10}},2^{\frac{m}{50}}]}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-\frac{3}{2}m+4H(1){\delta}m+\frac{m}{200}}2^{-k}2^{-\frac{k_{1}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(0)}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-\frac{5}{4}m}2^{-k}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+5}

and

\displaystyle\sum_{2^{k_{1}}\in[2^{-\frac{m}{10}},2^{\frac{m}{50}}]}\|\eqref{eq:s-mid-1}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2^{k_{1}}\in[2^{-\frac{m}{10}},2^{\frac{m}{50}}]}2^{-\frac{3}{2}k}2^{2H(1){\delta}m}\|\phi_{\theta_{1}}^{>J_{0},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|{P_{k_{2}}}\psi_{\theta_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2^{k_{1}}\in[2^{-\frac{m}{10}},2^{\frac{m}{50}}]}{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-2m+10H(1){\delta}m+\frac{m}{200}}2^{-k}2^{\frac{k_{2}}{2}}\left\langle 2^{k_{1}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)-1}\left\langle 2^{k_{2}}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+2}

\displaystyle\lesssim{\varepsilon}_{1}^{2}2^{-\frac{3}{2}m}2^{-k}\left\langle 2^{k}\right\rangle^{-N(1)+5}.

These lead us to (
9.10
). The estimates for (
9.12
) can be obtained by (
4.2
) similarly to those for (
9.11
).
